- REQ-CAPSTONE-4703: The retargeted held-out first-win readiness workflow SHALL write `results/experiment_4703_held_out_first_win_readiness.json` without submitting to the leaderboard and without modifying `scripts/research_conductor.py`. It SHALL first verify that `SUBMITTED_AGENT_CONFIG`/`E3AgentPolicy` and `experiment_4605_live_integration_scored_agent` are importable, SHALL run `tests/python/test_arc_submitted_agent_parity.py` before measuring the proxy, SHALL run the Exp4605 held-out scored-agent proxy over color-permuted variants with the literal submitted-agent config and no submit-time LLM, and SHALL compare `first_win_rate_integrated` against the last-submission baseline `0.04`. The readiness gate SHALL pass only when parity is green and either the held-out first-win improves with bootstrap CI support or the held-out first-win is flat with explicit null-delta markers (`null_delta_methodology_note` non-empty and `positive_control_passed=bool(parity_test_green AND no_regression_vs_baseline)`). It SHALL reproduce and record the replay package as a floor artifact only, SHALL explicitly mark replay count as not the leaderboard score, and SHALL never set readiness purely from `live_submittable_level_count`.

A flat first-win run must report `complete: held_out_first_win_flat_no_leaderboard_change`, emit the null-delta markers, set `positive_control_passed` only when parity and no-regression pass, and keep `replay_count_is_not_the_score=true` even when the replay package floor count exceeds 33.

- SCENARIO-CAPSTONE-4703-FIELD-PRINCIPLES: The required field principles are: `honest_verdict` = "terminal prefix; success: held_out_first_win_improved_ci_excludes_baseline OR complete: held_out_first_win_flat_no_leaderboard_change."; `inference_substrate` = "verifier_ensemble_against_cached_candidates -- the held-out proxy runs the scored agent over cached color-permuted variants offline, no live game server (1s floor)."; `verifier_is_oracle` = "MUST be false -- the held-out proxy measures the scored agent's first-win, oracle-distinct from the executable win-check."; `first_win_rate_integrated` = "the held-out generic first-win on color-permuted variants is the only offline proxy that tracks the scored leaderboard lane; the replay count does not."; `first_win_ci_lower` = "bootstrap-CI lower bound > baseline is the falsifiable improvement criterion; the exp4691 flat null was quarantined for omitting it)."; `positive_control_passed` = "bool(parity_test_green AND no_regression_vs_baseline) -- GATES the null-delta exemption so an UNVALIDATED flat null (parity failed / regression) is NOT excused (a fabricator cannot dodge by merely claiming no change)."; `multi_level_deepen_rate_integrated` = "deepening past L1 is the second scored lever; tracking it held-out keeps A4 honest about depth without using the replay count."; `parity_test_green` = "the held-out proxy is only valid if the measured agent is byte-for-byte the SUBMITTED_AGENT_CONFIG; a parity miss invalidates any readiness claim."; `replay_package_floor_reproduced` = "the replay package stays a reproduced FLOOR artifact, but its level count is explicitly NOT the leaderboard score (the retargeted framing)."; `replay_count_is_not_the_score` = "MUST be true -- explicitly records that live_submittable_level_count is NOT the leaderboard score (the retired dead-end framing)."; `ready_for_operator_submit` = "True if parity green AND the held-out first-win readiness gate passes; the task NEVER submits (operator-only)."; `random_seed` = "determinism precondition for reproducibility."; `reproducibility_checksum` = "content-addressed hash catches silent drift on replay."; `preconditions_checked` = "records resources verified (SUBMITTED_AGENT_CONFIG importable, experiment_4605 importable); pre-empts missing-resource fabrication."
